   188→# Set up and start Samba for SMB file sharing (SMB3-only, no NetBIOS).
   189→# Accessible via the sandbox's Tailscale IP on port 445: smb://<ip>/home or smb://<ip>/workspace
   190→SMB_ENABLED="${SANDCASTLE_SMB_ENABLED:-0}"
   191→if command -v smbd &>/dev/null && [ "$SMB_ENABLED" = "1" ]; then
   192→    cat > /etc/samba/smb.conf << SMBCONF
   193→[global]
   194→    disable netbios = yes
   195→    smb ports = 445
   196→    server min protocol = SMB3
   197→    server max protocol = SMB3_11
   198→    smb encrypt = if_required
   199→    server signing = required
   200→    map to guest = never
   201→    restrict anonymous = 2
   202→    load printers = no
   203→    printing = bsd
   204→    printcap name = /dev/null
   205→    disable spoolss = yes
   206→    log level = 1
   207→    log file = /var/log/samba/smbd.log
   208→
   209→[home]
   210→    path = /home/$USERNAME
   211→    browseable = yes
   212→    read only = no
   213→    valid users = $USERNAME
   214→    guest ok = no
   215→    create mask = 0644
   216→    directory mask = 0755
   217→SMBCONF
   218→
   219→    # Add workspace share if the persistent volume is mounted
   220→    if [ -d /workspace ]; then
   221→        cat >> /etc/samba/smb.conf << SMBCONF2
   222→
   223→[workspace]
   224→    path = /workspace
   225→    browseable = yes
   226→    read only = no
   227→    valid users = $USERNAME
   228→    guest ok = no
   229→    create mask = 0644
   230→    directory mask = 0755
   231→SMBCONF2
   232→    fi
   233→
   234→    mkdir -p /var/log/samba /run/samba
   235→    # SMB password is injected post-start via docker exec (not via env var)
   236→    touch /var/log/samba/smbd.log
   237→    smbd --foreground --no-process-group &>/var/log/samba/smbd.log &
   238→fi
   239→
   240→# Start SSH daemon in foreground
   241→exec /usr/sbin/sshd -D -e
   242→

    67→var createCmd = &cobra.Command{
    68→	Use:     "create [name]",
    69→	Aliases: []string{"cr"},
    70→	Short:   "Create a new sandbox",
    71→	Long: `Create a new sandbox.
    72→
    73→If no name is provided, creates a temporary sandbox with an auto-generated name like "temp-<timestamp>".
    74→
    75→Environment variables can set defaults for commonly used flags:
    76→  SANDCASTLE_HOME=1    equivalent to --home
    77→  SANDCASTLE_DATA=.    equivalent to --data (value is the subpath, "." or "1" for root)
    78→  SANDCASTLE_RM=1      equivalent to --rm
    79→
    80→Flags explicitly passed on the command line take precedence over environment variables.`,
    81→	Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
    82→	PreRun: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) {
    83→		// Priority: explicit flag > env var > config preference
    84→		cfg, _ := config.Load()
    85→		prefs := cfg.LoadPreferences()
    86→
    87→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("home") {
    88→			if prefs.MountHome != nil && *prefs.MountHome {
    89→				sandboxHome = true
    90→			}
    91→		}
    92→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("rm") && envTruthy("SANDCASTLE_RM") {
    93→			sandboxRemove = true
    94→		}
    95→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("data") {
    96→			if prefs.DataPath != "" {
    97→				sandboxData = prefs.DataPath
    98→			}
    99→		}
   100→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("no-vnc") {
   101→			if prefs.VNC != nil && !*prefs.VNC {
   102→				sandboxNoVNC = true
   103→			}
   104→		}
   105→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("no-docker") {
   106→			if prefs.Docker != nil && !*prefs.Docker {
   107→				sandboxNoDocker = true
   108→			}
   109→		}
   110→	},
   111→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   112→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   113→		if err != nil {
   114→			return err
   115→		}
   116→		printServer(client)
   117→
   118→		// Auto-generate name if not provided
   119→		var name string
   120→		autoGenerated := false
   121→		if len(args) == 0 {
   122→			name = fmt.Sprintf("temp-%d", time.Now().Unix())
   123→			autoGenerated = true
   124→			// Auto-generated sandboxes are temporary by default
   125→			if !cmd.Flags().Changed("rm") {
   126→				sandboxRemove = true
   127→			}
   128→		} else {
   129→			name = args[0]
   130→		}
   131→
   132→		// Resolve snapshot flags: --from-snapshot takes precedence over --snapshot
   133→		fromSnap := sandboxFromSnapshot
   134→		if fromSnap == "" {
   135→			fromSnap = sandboxSnapshot
   136→		}
   137→
   138→		var restoreLayers []string
   139→		if sandboxRestoreLayers != "" {
   140→			for _, l := range strings.Split(sandboxRestoreLayers, ",") {
   141→				restoreLayers = append(restoreLayers, strings.TrimSpace(l))
   142→			}
   143→		}
   144→
   145→		sandbox, err := client.CreateSandbox(api.CreateSandboxRequest{
   146→			Name:          name,
   147→			Image:         sandboxImage,
   148→			FromSnapshot:  fromSnap,
   149→			RestoreLayers: restoreLayers,
   150→			Tailscale:     sandboxTailscale,
   151→			MountHome:     sandboxHome,
   152→			DataPath:      sandboxData,
   153→			Temporary:     sandboxRemove,
   154→			VNCEnabled:    !sandboxNoVNC,
   155→			VNCGeometry:   sandboxVNCGeometry,
   156→			VNCDepth:      sandboxVNCDepth,
   157→			DockerEnabled: !sandboxNoDocker,
   158→			SMBEnabled:    sandboxSMB,
   159→		})
   160→		if err != nil {
   161→			return err
   162→		}
   163→
   164→		if autoGenerated {
   165→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q created (auto-generated name).\n", sandbox.Name)
   166→		} else {
   167→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q created.\n", sandbox.Name)
   168→		}
   169→
   170→		// Print active options (use local flags — they reflect what was actually requested)
   171→		if sandboxHome || sandboxData != "" || sandbox.Tailscale || sandboxRemove || fromSnap != "" || sandboxNoVNC || sandboxVNCGeometry != "" || sandboxVNCDepth != 0 || sandboxNoDocker || sandboxSMB {
   172→			if sandboxHome {
   173→				fmt.Println("  Home:      mounted (~/ persisted)")
   174→			}
   175→			if sandboxData != "" {

   177→				if label == "." {
   178→					label = "user data root"
   179→				}
   180→				fmt.Printf("  Data:      mounted (%s → /persisted)\n", label)
   181→			}

   184→			}

   187→			}

   190→			}
   191→			if sandboxNoDocker {

   203→					depth = 24
   204→				}
   205→				fmt.Printf("  VNC:       %s @ %d-bit\n", geom, depth)
   206→			}
   207→			if sandboxSMB {
   208→				fmt.Println("  SMB:       enabled")
   209→			}
   210→		}
   211→
   212→		if sandboxNoConnect {
   213→			return nil
   214→		}
   215→
   216→		info, err := client.ConnectInfo(sandbox.ID)
   217→		if err != nil {
   218→			return err
   219→		}
   220→
   221→		if os.Getenv("VERBOSE") == "1" {
   222→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] Connection info: host=%s port=%d user=%s\033[0m\n", info.Host, info.Port, info.User)
   223→			if info.TailscaleIP != "" {
   224→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] Tailscale IP (Tailscale): %s\033[0m\n", info.TailscaleIP)
   225→			}
   226→		}
   227→
   228→		if err := waitForSSH(info.Host, info.Port); err != nil {
   229→			return err
   230→		}
   231→
   232→		cfg, loadErr := config.Load()
   233→		if loadErr != nil {
   234→			return loadErr
   235→		}
   236→		prefs := cfg.LoadPreferences()
   237→
   238→		var remoteCmd string
   239→		if *prefs.UseTmux {
   240→			remoteCmd = tmuxCmd
   241→		}
   242→
   243→		var sshErr error
   244→		if pickProtocol(cfg, info.Host, info.Port, info.User, prefs.SSHExtraArgs) == "mosh" {
   245→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[33mWarning:\033[0m mosh does not support SSH agent forwarding. Use --mosh=no if you need ssh-add keys inside the sandbox.\n")
   246→			sshErr = moshExec(info.Host, info.Port, info.User, remoteCmd, prefs.SSHExtraArgs, nil)
   247→		} else {
   248→			sshErr = sshExec(info.Host, info.Port, info.User, remoteCmd, prefs.SSHExtraArgs, nil)
   249→		}
   250→
   251→		if sandboxRemove {
   252→			// Re-fetch to check if user toggled to "keep" during the session
   253→			current, fetchErr := client.GetSandbox(sandbox.ID)
   254→			if fetchErr == nil && !current.Temporary {
   255→				fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q was set to keep — skipping removal.\n", sandbox.Name)
   256→			} else {
   257→				fmt.Printf("Removing sandbox %q...\n", sandbox.Name)
   258→				if err := client.DestroySandbox(sandbox.ID); err != nil {
   259→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to delete sandbox: %v\n", err)
   260→				} else {
   261→					fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q deleted.\n", sandbox.Name)
   262→				}
   263→			}
   264→		}
   265→
   266→		return sshErr
   267→	},
   268→}

   479→var useCmd = &cobra.Command{
   480→	Use:   "use [name]",
   481→	Short: "Show or set active server/sandbox",
   482→	Long: `Without arguments, shows the current server and active sandbox.
   483→With an argument, switches the active server or sandbox.
   484→
   485→Examples:
   486→  sandcastle use                  # Show current server and sandbox
   487→  sandcastle use my-sandbox       # Set active sandbox
   488→  sandcastle use prod             # Switch to server "prod" (if configured)`,
   489→	Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
   490→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   491→		cfg, err := config.Load()
   492→		if err != nil {
   493→			return err
   494→		}
   495→
   496→		// No args: list all servers, highlight active
   497→		if len(args) == 0 {
   498→			if len(cfg.Servers) == 0 {
   499→				fmt.Println("No servers configured — run: sandcastle login <url>")
   500→				return nil
   501→			}
   502→
   503→			aliases := make([]string, 0, len(cfg.Servers))
   504→			for alias := range cfg.Servers {
   505→				aliases = append(aliases, alias)
   506→			}
   507→			sort.Strings(aliases)
   508→
   509→			for _, alias := range aliases {
   510→				srv := cfg.Servers[alias]
   511→				if alias == cfg.CurrentServer {
   512→					fmt.Printf("  \033[1m* %-16s\033[0m %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   513→				} else {
   514→					fmt.Printf("    %-16s %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   515→				}
   516→			}
   517→
   518→			return nil
   519→		}
   520→
   521→		name := args[0]
   522→
   523→		// Check if name matches a server alias or URL
   524→		if _, ok := cfg.Servers[name]; ok {
   525→			cfg.CurrentServer = name
   526→			if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   527→				return err
   528→			}
   529→			fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", name, cfg.Servers[name].URL)
   530→			return nil
   531→		}
   532→
   533→		// Try matching by URL
   534→		for alias, srv := range cfg.Servers {
   535→			if srv.URL == strings.TrimRight(name, "/") {
   536→				cfg.CurrentServer = alias
   537→				if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   538→					return err
   539→				}
   540→				fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", alias, srv.URL)
   541→				return nil
   542→			}
   543→		}
   544→
   545→		return fmt.Errorf("server %q not found — run: sandcastle use", name)
   546→	},
   547→}

   621→
   622→// formatImageAge returns a human-readable image age string (e.g. "2h ago", "3d ago").
   623→// Returns "-" when the build timestamp is not available.
   624→func formatImageAge(builtAt *time.Time) string {
   625→	if builtAt == nil {
   626→		return "-"
   627→	}
   628→	d := time.Since(*builtAt)
   629→	switch {
   630→	case d < time.Minute:
   631→		return "just now"
   632→	case d < time.Hour:
   633→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dm ago", int(d.Minutes()))
   634→	case d < 24*time.Hour:
   635→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dh ago", int(d.Hours()))
   636→	default:
   637→		return fmt.Sprintf("%dd ago", int(d.Hours()/24))
   638→	}
   639→}
   640→
   641→func envTruthy(key string) bool {
   642→	v := strings.ToLower(os.Getenv(key))
   643→	return v == "1" || v == "true" || v == "yes"
   644→}
   645→
   646→func findSandboxByName(client *api.Client, name string) (*api.Sandbox, error) {
   647→	sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
   648→	if err != nil {
   649→		return nil, err
   650→	}
   651→	for _, s := range sandboxes {
   652→		if s.Name == name {
   653→			return &s, nil
   654→		}
   655→	}
   656→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("sandbox %q not found", name)
   657→}

```
     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→# Wrapper for tmux that preserves SSH agent forwarding across sessions.
     3→#
     4→# When SSH connects with -A, sshd creates a temporary agent socket.
     5→# We symlink a stable path to it so tmux sessions always find the
     6→# latest forwarded agent — even on reattach.
     7→
     8→if [ -n "$SSH_AUTH_SOCK" ] && [ -S "$SSH_AUTH_SOCK" ]; then
     9→    mkdir -p ~/.ssh 2>/dev/null
    10→    ln -sf "$SSH_AUTH_SOCK" ~/.ssh/agent_sock
    11→    export SSH_AUTH_SOCK=~/.ssh/agent_sock
    12→fi
    13→
    14→exec tmux new-session -A -s main
    15→
```

    26→    def create
    27→      authorize Sandbox
    28→
    29→      manager = SandboxManager.new
    30→
    31→      # Resolve snapshot for container image
    32→      from_snapshot_name = params[:from_snapshot].presence || params[:snapshot].presence
    33→      restore_layers     = params[:restore_layers].present? ? Array(params[:restore_layers]) : nil
    34→
    35→      image = if from_snapshot_name.present?
    36→        # Try to find DB record first
    37→        snap = Snapshot.find_by(user: current_user, name: from_snapshot_name)
    38→        snap&.docker_image || "sc-snap-#{current_user.name}:#{from_snapshot_name}"
    39→      else
    40→        params[:image].presence || SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE
    41→      end
    42→
    43→      # Build sandbox record (use system defaults where not explicitly provided)
    44→      defaults = Setting.instance
    45→      sandbox = current_user.sandboxes.build(
    46→        name: params.require(:name),
    47→        status: "pending",
    48→        image: image,
    49→        mount_home: params.key?(:mount_home) ? params[:mount_home] : defaults.default_mount_home,
    50→        data_path: params.key?(:data_path) ? params[:data_path] : defaults.default_data_path,
    51→        tailscale: params.fetch(:tailscale) { current_user.tailscale_enabled? },
    52→        vnc_enabled: params.key?(:vnc_enabled) ? params[:vnc_enabled] : defaults.default_vnc_enabled,
    53→        vnc_geometry: params[:vnc_geometry] || "1280x900",
    54→        vnc_depth: params[:vnc_depth]&.to_i || 24,
    55→        docker_enabled: params.key?(:docker_enabled) ? params[:docker_enabled] : defaults.default_docker_enabled,
    56→        temporary: params[:temporary] || false,
    57→        smb_enabled: params[:smb_enabled] || false
    58→      )
    59→
    60→      sandbox.save!
    61→
    62→      # Restore BTRFS layers from snapshot (if requested and available)
    63→      if from_snapshot_name.present? && snap.present?
    64→        want_home = restore_layers.nil? || restore_layers.include?("home")
    65→        want_data = restore_layers.nil? || restore_layers.include?("data")
    66→
    67→        if want_home && snap.home_snapshot.present? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
    68→          manager.ensure_mount_dirs(current_user, sandbox)
    69→          home_target = "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/users/#{current_user.name}/home"
    70→          BtrfsHelper.restore_subvolume(snap.home_snapshot, home_target) rescue nil
    71→        end
    72→
    73→        if want_data && snap.data_snapshot.present? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
    74→          data_target = if snap.data_subdir.present? && sandbox.data_path.present?
    75→            "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/users/#{current_user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}/#{snap.data_subdir}".chomp("/")
    76→          elsif sandbox.data_path.present?
    77→            "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/users/#{current_user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
    78→          end
    79→          BtrfsHelper.restore_subvolume(snap.data_snapshot, data_target) rescue nil if data_target
    80→        end
    81→      end
    82→
    83→      # Enqueue async job
    84→      SandboxProvisionJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: sandbox.id)
    85→
    86→      # Return immediately with job_status so CLI can poll
    87→      render json: sandbox_json(sandbox), status: :created
    88→    end

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01ETiDwMAe128qqKa4TRLdjr
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh",
  "old_string": "    # Add workspace share if the persistent volume is mounted\n    if [ -d /workspace ]; then\n        cat >> /etc/samba/smb.conf << SMBCONF2\n\n[workspace]\n    path = /workspace\n    browseable = yes\n    read only = no\n    valid users = $USERNAME\n    guest ok = no\n    create mask = 0644\n    directory mask = 0755\nSMBCONF2\n    fi",
  "new_string": "    # Add persisted data share if the volume is mounted\n    if [ -d /persisted ]; then\n        cat >> /etc/samba/smb.conf << SMBCONF2\n\n[persisted]\n    path = /persisted\n    browseable = yes\n    read only = no\n    valid users = $USERNAME\n    guest ok = no\n    create mask = 0644\n    directory mask = 0755\nSMBCONF2\n    fi\n\n    # Legacy: add workspace share if mounted (old volume path)\n    if [ -d /workspace ] && [ ! -d /persisted ]; then\n        cat >> /etc/samba/smb.conf << SMBCONF3\n\n[workspace]\n    path = /workspace\n    browseable = yes\n    read only = no\n    valid users = $USERNAME\n    guest ok = no\n    create mask = 0644\n    directory mask = 0755\nSMBCONF3\n    fi"
}
```

> AGENT

All changes look good. Here's what was changed:

1. **Form UI** (`new.html.erb`): Data path is now a "Mount /persisted" toggle checkbox. When enabled, an optional subpath field appears. Empty subpath defaults to `.` (root data dir).

2. **Controller** (`sandboxes_controller.rb`): `mount_data` checkbox drives `data_path` — checked with no subpath = `"."`, checked with subpath = that subpath, unchecked = `nil`.

3. **SMB** (`entrypoint.sh`): Exports `/persisted` as an SMB share when the data volume is mounted. Legacy `/workspace` kept as fallback.

4. **Connect** (`sc-tmux.sh`): Added `cd ~` before tmux exec so you land in `~/` on connect.

5. **Guide + CLI help**: Updated references from `/workspace` to `/persisted`.

id: toolu_01EQpEJXtccwH8CAo28jUWw2
```
     1→# Sandcastle Snapshots
     2→
     3→Snapshots capture the state of a sandbox at a point in time. They are used for backup, rollback, branching workflows, and distributing pre-configured environments.
     4→
     5→## Contents
     6→
     7→- [Concepts](#concepts)
     8→- [Layers](#layers)
     9→- [Storage Layout](#storage-layout)
    10→- [Lifecycle](#lifecycle)
    11→- [CLI Reference](#cli-reference)
    12→- [Creating a New Sandbox from a Snapshot](#creating-a-new-sandbox-from-a-snapshot)
    13→- [Restore vs Clone](#restore-vs-clone)
    14→- [Legacy Snapshots](#legacy-snapshots)
    15→- [Internals](#internals)
    16→
    17→---
    18→
    19→## Concepts
    20→
    21→A **snapshot** is a named, user-scoped record that captures one or more *layers* of a sandbox:
    22→
    23→| Layer | What it captures | Technology | Requires |
    24→|---|---|---|---|
    25→| `container` | Everything installed inside the sandbox (OS, packages, files in `/`) | `docker commit` | Always available |
    26→| `home` | The user's home directory (`/home/<user>`) | BTRFS subvolume snapshot | BTRFS filesystem + `mount_home` sandbox option |
    27→| `data` | The shared data mount (`/workspace/data`) | BTRFS subvolume snapshot | BTRFS filesystem + `data_path` sandbox option |
    28→| `workspace` | The persistent volume (`/workspace`) | BTRFS subvolume snapshot | BTRFS filesystem + `persistent` sandbox option |
    29→
    30→Every snapshot has at least a `container` layer. BTRFS layers are added automatically when the filesystem supports it and the sandbox has those mounts configured.
    31→
    32→```mermaid
    33→graph TD
    34→    SB["Sandbox\n(running container)"]
    35→    SB --> CL["container layer\ndocker commit → sc-snap-user:name"]
    36→    SB --> HL["home layer\nBTRFS snapshot\n/data/snapshots/user/name/home"]
    37→    SB --> DL["data layer\nBTRFS snapshot\n/data/snapshots/user/name/data"]
    38→
    39→    CL --> SNAP["Snapshot record\n(DB)"]
    40→    HL --> SNAP
    41→    DL --> SNAP
    42→
    43→    style CL fill:#dbeafe
    44→    style HL fill:#dcfce7
    45→    style DL fill:#fef9c3
    46→    style SNAP fill:#f3e8ff
    47→```
    48→
    49→---
    50→
    51→## Layers
    52→
    53→### Container layer
    54→
    55→Captured via `docker commit`, which freezes the container's writable filesystem layer into a new Docker image. The image is tagged as:
    56→
    57→```
    58→sc-snap-<username>:<snapshot-name>
    59→```
    60→
    61→This image is stored in the local Docker daemon (not pushed to any registry). It captures everything installed in `/` — packages, configuration, compiled binaries, files written as root — but does **not** capture:
    62→
    63→- The user's home directory if it is bind-mounted from the host (`mount_home`)
    64→- Any bind-mounted data paths
    65→
    66→### Home layer (BTRFS only)
    67→
    68→When the sandbox uses `mount_home: true`, the user's home directory at `/data/users/<username>/home` is a BTRFS subvolume. The snapshot creates a read-write BTRFS snapshot at:
    69→
    70→```
    71→/data/snapshots/<username>/<snapshot-name>/home
    72→```
    73→
    74→BTRFS snapshots are copy-on-write: they are instant and initially share all disk blocks with the source subvolume.
    75→
    76→### Data layer (BTRFS only)
    77→
    78→When the sandbox has a `data_path` configured, the data directory at `/data/users/<username>/data/<data_path>` is snapshotted to:
    79→
    80→```
    81→/data/snapshots/<username>/<snapshot-name>/data
    82→```
    83→
    84→The `--data-subdir` flag allows snapshotting only a subdirectory of the data mount, which is useful for large data volumes where only part needs to be captured.
    85→
    86→### Workspace layer (BTRFS only)
    87→
    88→When the sandbox has a persistent volume (`persistent: true`), the workspace at `/data/sandboxes/<sandbox>/vol` is snapshotted to:
    89→
    90→```
    91→/data/snapshots/<username>/<snapshot-name>/workspace
    92→```
    93→
    94→This is stored in the `data_snapshot` database column (shared with the data layer — only one data-side path is stored per snapshot).
    95→
    96→---
    97→
    98→## Storage Layout
    99→
   100→```
   101→/data/
   102→├── snapshots/
   103→│   └── <username>/
   104→│       └── <snapshot-name>/
   105→│           ├── home/          ← BTRFS snapshot of home subvolume
   106→│           └── data/          ← BTRFS snapshot of data/workspace subvolume
   107→│
   108→└── (Docker daemon stores container layer images internally)
   109→     sc-snap-<username>:<snapshot-name>
   110→```
   111→
   112→The `snapshots` database table holds metadata for all layers:
   113→
   114→| Column | Description |
   115→|---|---|
   116→| `name` | Snapshot name (unique per user) |
   117→| `label` | Optional human-readable description |
   118→| `source_sandbox` | Name of the sandbox this was taken from |
   119→| `docker_image` | Docker image ref (`sc-snap-user:name`) |
   120→| `docker_size` | Size of the Docker image in bytes |
   121→| `home_snapshot` | Absolute path to BTRFS home snapshot (if present) |
   122→| `home_size` | Size of home snapshot in bytes |
   123→| `data_snapshot` | Absolute path to BTRFS data/workspace snapshot (if present) |
   124→| `data_size` | Size of data snapshot in bytes |
   125→| `data_subdir` | Subdir of data mount that was snapshotted (optional) |
   126→
   127→---
   128→
   129→## Lifecycle
   130→
   131→### Create
   132→
   133→```mermaid
   134→sequenceDiagram
   135→    participant CLI as CLI / Web UI
   136→    participant API as Rails API
   137→    participant Docker as Docker daemon
   138→    participant BTRFS as BTRFS (host)
   139→    participant DB as PostgreSQL
   140→
   141→    CLI->>API: POST /api/sandboxes/:id/snapshot\n{name, label, layers}
   142→    API->>Docker: container.commit(repo: "sc-snap-user", tag: name)
   143→    Docker-->>API: image ref + size
   144→    API->>BTRFS: snapshot_subvolume(home_src → home_dest)
   145→    API->>BTRFS: snapshot_subvolume(data_src → data_dest)
   146→    API->>DB: INSERT INTO snapshots (...)
   147→    API-->>CLI: {name, layers, docker_image, ...}
   148→```
   149→
   150→All three steps (Docker commit, BTRFS home, BTRFS data) are attempted. If BTRFS is not available or the sandbox does not have those mounts, only the container layer is captured.
   151→
   152→---
   153→
   154→### Restore (in-place)
   155→
   156→Restores an existing sandbox back to a snapshot's state. The sandbox is **replaced** in-place: same ID, same SSH port, same config — new container.
   157→
   158→```mermaid
   159→sequenceDiagram
   160→    participant CLI as CLI / Web UI
   161→    participant API as Rails API
   162→    participant Docker as Docker daemon
   163→    participant BTRFS as BTRFS (host)
   164→    participant DB as PostgreSQL
   165→
   166→    CLI->>API: POST /api/sandboxes/:id/restore\n{snapshot_name, layers}
   167→    API->>API: Close terminal + VNC sidecars
   168→    API->>API: Disconnect Tailscale (if connected)
   169→    API->>Docker: Stop + delete old container
   170→    API->>BTRFS: restore_subvolume(home_snap → home_dir)
   171→    API->>BTRFS: restore_subvolume(data_snap → data_dir)
   172→    API->>Docker: Create new container from snapshot image
   173→    API->>Docker: Start container
   174→    API->>DB: UPDATE sandboxes SET container_id, image, status
   175→    API->>API: Reconnect Tailscale (if was connected)
   176→    API-->>CLI: updated sandbox object
   177→```
   178→
   179→> **Warning:** Restore is destructive. Any changes made to the sandbox since the snapshot was taken are permanently lost.
   180→
   181→---
   182→
   183→### Clone (new sandbox from snapshot)
   184→
   185→Creates a **new sandbox** pre-loaded with a snapshot's state. The original sandbox is untouched.
   186→
   187→```mermaid
   188→sequenceDiagram
   189→    participant CLI as CLI / Web UI
   190→    participant API as Rails API
   191→    participant Docker as Docker daemon
   192→    participant BTRFS as BTRFS (host)
   193→    participant DB as PostgreSQL
   194→
   195→    CLI->>API: POST /api/sandboxes\n{name, from_snapshot: "v1", restore_layers: [...]}
   196→    API->>Docker: Image.get("sc-snap-user:v1")
   197→    Note over API,Docker: ensure_image checks local first,\npulls only if not found
   198→    API->>BTRFS: restore_subvolume(home_snap → new home_dir)
   199→    API->>BTRFS: restore_subvolume(data_snap → new data_dir)
   200→    API->>Docker: Create container from snapshot image
   201→    API->>Docker: Start container
   202→    API->>DB: INSERT INTO sandboxes (new record)
   203→    API-->>CLI: new sandbox object
   204→```
   205→
   206→---
   207→
   208→### Destroy
   209→
   210→```mermaid
   211→flowchart TD
   212→    D["destroy_snapshot(user, name)"]
   213→    D --> FIND{"DB record\nexists?"}
   214→
   215→    FIND -->|yes| RM_IMG["Remove Docker image\nsc-snap-user:name"]
   216→    RM_IMG --> RM_HOME{"home_snapshot\npresent?"}
   217→    RM_HOME -->|yes| DEL_HOME["BTRFS delete\nhome snapshot"]
   218→    RM_HOME -->|no| RM_DATA
   219→    DEL_HOME --> RM_DATA{"data_snapshot\npresent?"}
   220→    RM_DATA -->|yes| DEL_DATA["BTRFS delete\ndata snapshot"]
   221→    RM_DATA -->|no| DEL_DB
   222→    DEL_DATA --> DEL_DB["DELETE FROM snapshots"]
   223→
   224→    FIND -->|no| LEGACY["Legacy fallback:\nremove sc-snap-user:name\nDocker image directly"]
   225→```
   226→
   227→---
   228→
   229→## CLI Reference
   230→
   231→### Create a snapshot
   232→
   233→```bash
   234→# Container layer only (default for legacy endpoint)
   235→sandcastle snapshot create my-sandbox v1
   236→
   237→# All available layers with a label
   238→sandcastle snapshot create my-sandbox v1 --label "after ruby setup"
   239→
   240→# Specific layers only
   241→sandcastle snapshot create my-sandbox v1 --layers container,home
   242→
   243→# Snapshot only a subdirectory of the data mount
   244→sandcastle snapshot create my-sandbox v1 --data-subdir projects
   245→```
   246→
   247→### List snapshots
   248→
   249→```bash
   250→sandcastle snapshot list
   251→# or
   252→sandcastle snapshot ls
   253→```
   254→
   255→Output:
   256→```
   257→NAME    SOURCE          LAYERS              SIZE        CREATED
   258→v1      my-sandbox      container home      1.2 GB      2026-02-20 19:00
   259→nightly quantum-phoenix  container           3.4 GB      2026-02-19 13:37
   260→```
   261→
   262→### Show snapshot details
   263→
   264→```bash
   265→sandcastle snapshot show v1
   266→```
   267→
   268→```
   269→Snapshot:       v1
   270→Label:          after ruby setup
   271→Source sandbox: my-sandbox
   272→Created:        2026-02-20 19:00:19
   273→
   274→Layers:
   275→  container    sc-snap-thies:v1                                    1.2 GB
   276→  home         (BTRFS snapshot)                                    45.0 MB
   277→
   278→Total:         1.2 GB
   279→```
   280→
   281→### Restore a sandbox to a snapshot
   282→
   283→```bash
   284→# Restore all layers
   285→sandcastle snapshot restore my-sandbox v1
   286→
   287→# Restore only the container layer (keep current home/data)
   288→sandcastle snapshot restore my-sandbox v1 --layers container
   289→```
   290→
   291→### Create a new sandbox from a snapshot
   292→
   293→```bash
   294→# Clone with all layers
   295→sandcastle sandbox create my-sandbox-clone --from-snapshot v1
   296→
   297→# Clone container layer only
   298→sandcastle sandbox create my-sandbox-clone --from-snapshot v1 --restore-layers container
   299→```
   300→
   301→### Delete a snapshot
   302→
   303→```bash
   304→sandcastle snapshot destroy v1
   305→# or
   306→sandcastle snapshot delete v1
   307→```
   308→
   309→---
   310→
   311→## Restore vs Clone
   312→
   313→| | Restore | Clone |
   314→|---|---|---|
   315→| Target | Existing sandbox | New sandbox |
   316→| Original sandbox | Replaced | Untouched |
   317→| ID / SSH port | Preserved | New |
   318→| Use case | Rollback | Branching, environment distribution |
   319→| CLI | `snapshot restore <sandbox> <snap>` | `sandbox create --from-snapshot <snap>` |
   320→| API | `POST /api/sandboxes/:id/restore` | `POST /api/sandboxes` with `from_snapshot` |
   321→
   322→```mermaid
   323→graph LR
   324→    SNAP["Snapshot v1"]
   325→
   326→    SNAP -->|restore| SB_SAME["Same sandbox\n(replaced in-place)"]
   327→    SNAP -->|clone| SB_NEW1["New sandbox A"]
   328→    SNAP -->|clone| SB_NEW2["New sandbox B"]
   329→
   330→    style SNAP fill:#f3e8ff
   331→    style SB_SAME fill:#fee2e2
   332→    style SB_NEW1 fill:#dcfce7
   333→    style SB_NEW2 fill:#dcfce7
   334→```
   335→
   336→---
   337→
   338→## Legacy Snapshots
   339→
   340→Before the `snapshots` table was introduced, snapshots were stored only as Docker images tagged `sc-snap-<user>:<name>`. These are imported automatically into the database the first time they are accessed (via `import_legacy_snapshots`). The import is idempotent — it only creates new DB records for images that have no existing record.
   341→
   342→Legacy snapshots have only a `container` layer; they have no `home_snapshot` or `data_snapshot`.
   343→
   344→---
   345→
   346→## Internals
   347→
   348→### `SandboxManager#create_snapshot`
   349→
   350→The primary API. Accepts `layers:` to selectively capture a subset. When `layers:` is `nil`, defaults to all four (`container`, `home`, `data`, `workspace`) and skips silently for any that are not applicable.
   351→
   352→### `SandboxManager#snapshot` (legacy alias)
   353→
   354→Wraps `create_snapshot` with `layers: %w[container]` only. Used by the original CLI snapshot endpoint and kept for backward compatibility.
   355→
   356→### `ensure_image`
   357→
   358→Before starting a sandbox from a snapshot image, `ensure_image` checks `Docker::Image.get` first. Only if the image is not found locally does it attempt a remote pull. This is critical because snapshot images are local (`sc-snap-*`) and are never pushed to a registry — attempting to pull them would always fail.
   359→
   360→### BTRFS copy-on-write semantics
   361→
   362→BTRFS snapshots are instant and space-efficient. At the moment of creation, the snapshot shares all disk blocks with the source subvolume. Only blocks written after the snapshot diverge. This means:
   363→
   364→- Creating a snapshot of a 10 GB home directory is near-instantaneous
   365→- The snapshot initially consumes ~0 additional disk space
   366→- Disk usage grows only as the live subvolume or the snapshot diverge
   367→
   368→`BtrfsHelper.subvolume_size` reports the exclusive bytes used by the snapshot (blocks not shared with any other subvolume).
   369→
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/docs/SNAPSHOTS.md",
  "old_string": "| `data` | The shared data mount (`/workspace/data`) | BTRFS subvolume snapshot | BTRFS filesystem + `data_path` sandbox option |\n| `workspace` | The persistent volume (`/workspace`) | BTRFS subvolume snapshot | BTRFS filesystem + `persistent` sandbox option |",
  "new_string": "| `data` | The persistent data mount (`/persisted`) | BTRFS subvolume snapshot | BTRFS filesystem + `data_path` sandbox option |"
}
```

   160→### Q: "Can't we just use Kata for stronger security?"

   195→Sandcastle uses bind mounts for:

```
     1→# Installer Build System
     2→
     3→The `installer.sh` is **generated** from `installer.sh.in` template and source files.
     4→
     5→## How It Works
     6→
     7→```
     8→installer.sh.in              # Template with @@TEMPLATE:file@@ markers
     9→  + templates/*.yml          # Static config templates
    10→  + docker-compose.yml.template  # Parameterized compose file
    11→  + /docker/postgres/init-databases.sh  # Source file from repo
    12→  ↓
    13→  installer/build.sh         # Build script
    14→  ↓
    15→installer.sh                 # Generated installer (committed to repo)
    16→```
    17→
    18→## Quick Start
    19→
    20→**Build installer.sh from template:**
    21→```bash
    22→mise run installer:build
    23→```
    24→
    25→**Validate installer.sh is up to date:**
    26→```bash
    27→mise run installer:validate
    28→```
    29→
    30→## File Structure
    31→
    32→```
    33→installer/
    34→  installer.sh.in           # Template with @@TEMPLATE:markers@@
    35→  build.sh                  # Generates installer.sh from template
    36→  templates/                # Template files
    37→    banner.sh               # Login banner for sandboxes
    38→    docker-compose.yml.template  # Parameterized compose file
    39→    docker-logs.sh          # Wrapper script
    40→    dockyard.env.template   # Dockyard config
    41→    sandcastle.env.template # User config template
    42→    traefik-*.yml           # Traefik configs (letsencrypt/selfsigned)
    43→    rails-*.yml             # Initial Rails routes
    44→```
    45→
    46→## Workflow
    47→
    48→### Modifying installer logic
    49→
    50→1. Edit `installer/installer.sh.in`
    51→2. Run `mise run installer:build`
    52→3. Test the generated `installer.sh`
    53→4. Commit both files
    54→
    55→### Modifying embedded files
    56→
    57→**For template files (banner, traefik configs, etc.):**
    58→1. Edit the file in `installer/templates/`
    59→2. Run `mise run installer:build`
    60→3. Commit both the template and generated installer.sh
    61→
    62→**For source files (init-databases.sh):**
    63→1. Edit `/docker/postgres/init-databases.sh`
    64→2. Run `mise run installer:build`
    65→3. Commit both files
    66→
    67→**For docker-compose.yml:**
    68→1. Edit `/docker-compose.yml` for local dev
    69→2. Edit `installer/templates/docker-compose.yml.template` for deployment
    70→3. Key differences:
    71→   - Template uses `${APP_IMAGE}`, `${DATA_MOUNT}`, `${SANDCASTLE_HOME}`
    72→   - Template escapes variables: `\${DB_PASSWORD}` (literal $ in output)
    73→   - Template adds `runtime: runc` to services
    74→4. Run `mise run installer:build`
    75→5. Commit all three files
    76→
    77→## Template Markers
    78→
    79→In `installer.sh.in`, heredoc content is replaced with:
    80→```bash
    81→cat > "$file" <<MARKER
    82→@@TEMPLATE:filename@@
    83→MARKER
    84→```
    85→
    86→The build script replaces `@@TEMPLATE:filename@@` with the file contents.
    87→
    88→Supported formats:
    89→- `@@TEMPLATE:banner.sh@@` - from `installer/templates/banner.sh`
    90→- `@@TEMPLATE:templates/file.yml@@` - explicit path
    91→- `@@TEMPLATE:/docker/postgres/init.sh@@` - from repo root
    92→
    93→## Pre-commit Hook
    94→
    95→Add to `.git/hooks/pre-commit`:
    96→```bash
    97→#!/bin/bash
    98→if ! mise run installer:validate; then
    99→  echo "ERROR: Run 'mise run installer:build' before committing"
   100→  exit 1
   101→fi
   102→```
   103→
   104→## Why This System?
   105→
   106→**Problem:** installer.sh embedded many files as heredocs, which got out of sync with source files (e.g., docker-compose.yml, init-databases.sh).
   107→
   108→**Solution:**
   109→- Single source of truth for each file
   110→- Template-based generation prevents drift
   111→- Validation task catches sync issues
   112→
   113→## Migration Notes
   114→
   115→Old system (pre-refactor):
   116→- `installer.sh` - monolithic file with embedded heredocs
   117→- Manual sync required when changing embedded files
   118→- Frequent drift between dev and deployment configs
   119→
   120→New system:
   121→- `installer.sh.in` - template with markers
   122→- `installer/build.sh` - automatic generation
   123→- `mise run installer:build` - single command to rebuild
   124→
```

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→# Build installer.sh from installer.sh.in template
     3→set -euo pipefail
     4→
     5→SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
     6→REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"
     7→TEMPLATES_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/templates"
     8→TEMPLATE_FILE="$SCRIPT_DIR/installer.sh.in"
     9→OUTPUT_FILE="$REPO_ROOT/installer.sh"
    10→
    11→# Colors for output
    12→RED='\033[0;31m'
    13→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
    14→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
    15→NC='\033[0m' # No Color
    16→
    17→info() { echo -e "${GREEN}→${NC} $*" >&2; }
    18→warn() { echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠${NC} $*" >&2; }
    19→error() { echo -e "${RED}✗${NC} $*" >&2; }
    20→ok() { echo -e "${GREEN}✓${NC} $*" >&2; }
    21→
    22→if [ ! -f "$TEMPLATE_FILE" ]; then
    23→  error "Template file not found: $TEMPLATE_FILE"
    24→  error "Run: cp installer.sh installer/installer.sh.in"
    25→  error "Then replace heredocs with @@TEMPLATE:filename@@ markers"
    26→  exit 1
    27→fi
    28→
    29→info "Building installer.sh from template..."
    30→
    31→# Create temporary output file
    32→TEMP_OUTPUT=$(mktemp)
    33→
    34→# Process the template line by line
    35→while IFS= read -r line; do
    36→  # Check for template markers: @@TEMPLATE:filename@@
    37→  if [[ "$line" =~ @@TEMPLATE:([^@]+)@@ ]]; then
    38→    template_file="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
    39→
    40→    # Determine the full path to the template
    41→    if [[ "$template_file" == /* ]]; then
    42→      # Absolute path from repo root
    43→      full_path="$REPO_ROOT/${template_file#/}"
    44→    elif [[ "$template_file" == templates/* ]]; then
    45→      # Relative to installer/templates/
    46→      full_path="$TEMPLATES_DIR/${template_file#templates/}"
    47→    else
    48→      # Assume it's in templates/
    49→      full_path="$TEMPLATES_DIR/$template_file"
    50→    fi
    51→
    52→    if [ ! -f "$full_path" ]; then
    53→      error "Template file not found: $full_path"
    54→      rm -f "$TEMP_OUTPUT"
    55→      exit 1
    56→    fi
    57→
    58→    info "  Injecting: $template_file"
    59→    cat "$full_path"
    60→  else
    61→    # Regular line, output as-is
    62→    echo "$line"
    63→  fi
    64→done < "$TEMPLATE_FILE" > "$TEMP_OUTPUT"
    65→
    66→# Move to final location
    67→mv "$TEMP_OUTPUT" "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    68→chmod +x "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    69→
    70→ok "Built: $OUTPUT_FILE"
    71→echo ""
    72→info "Verify the output and test before committing!"
    73→
```

index e00f078..59db057 100644
--- a/installer/templates/dockyard.sh
+++ b/installer/templates/dockyard.sh
@@ -144,6 +144,48 @@ cleanup_pool_bridges() {
     [ "$removed" -gt 0 ] || true
 }
 
+# Detect the backing filesystem type for the given data directory.
+# Returns the fstype string (e.g., "ext4", "zfs", "xfs").
+detect_backing_fs() {
+    local data_dir="$1"
+
+    # Walk up to the nearest existing directory
+    local check_dir="$data_dir"
+    while [ ! -d "$check_dir" ]; do
+        check_dir="$(dirname "$check_dir")"
+    done
+
+    df --output=fstype "$check_dir" 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | tr -d '[:space:]'
+}
+
+# Detect the optimal Docker storage driver for the given data directory.
+# Always returns "overlay2" — sysbox-runc does not support ZFS as a container
+# rootfs filesystem (fails with "unknown fs"). overlay2 works on ZFS 2.2+ and
+# on all other common Linux filesystems.
+# Can be overridden with DOCKYARD_STORAGE_DRIVER for future use.
+detect_storage_driver() {
+    local data_dir="$1"
+
+    # Manual override
+    if [ -n "${DOCKYARD_STORAGE_DRIVER:-}" ]; then
+        case "$DOCKYARD_STORAGE_DRIVER" in
+            auto)   ;; # fall through to detection
+            overlay2|zfs)
+                echo "$DOCKYARD_STORAGE_DRIVER"
+                return
+                ;;
+            *)
+                echo "Error: unsupported DOCKYARD_STORAGE_DRIVER=${DOCKYARD_STORAGE_DRIVER} (use auto, overlay2, or zfs)" >&2
+                exit 1
+                ;;
+        esac
+    fi
+
+    # sysbox requires overlay2 — it does not recognize ZFS rootfs.
+    # overlay2 works on ZFS 2.2+ with overlayfs kernel support.
+    echo "overlay2"
+}
+
 wait_for_file() {
     local file="$1"
     local label="$2"
@@ -646,6 +688,15 @@ DOCKEREOF
 
     echo "Installed binaries to ${BIN_DIR}/"
 
+    # Detect storage driver and backing filesystem.
+    # sysbox requires overlay2 — ZFS native driver causes "unknown fs" errors.
+    # overlay2 works on ZFS 2.2+ with overlayfs kernel support.
+    local STORAGE_DRIVER BACKING_FS
+    STORAGE_DRIVER=$(detect_storage_driver "$DOCKER_DATA")
+    BACKING_FS=$(detect_backing_fs "$DOCKER_DATA")
+    echo "  storage:     ${STORAGE_DRIVER} (on ${BACKING_FS})"
+    echo ""
+
     # Write daemon.json (embedded — no external file dependency)

     # runtimeArgs works correctly. No wrapper script needed.

 StartLimitIntervalSec=60
 
@@ -1338,8 +1390,35 @@ cmd_destroy() {
             echo "Removed instance files from ${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/"
             echo "Data preserved at ${DOCKER_DATA}"
         else
-            rm -rf "$DOCKYARD_ROOT"
-            echo "Removed ${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/"
+            # When the data-root sits on a ZFS dataset, Docker (with overlay2)
+            # may have created overlay dirs that need normal removal, and
+            # rm -rf on a ZFS mountpoint removes contents but can't remove
+            # the mountpoint directory itself.
+            local on_zfs=false
+            if command -v zfs &>/dev/null; then
+                local root_dataset
+                root_dataset="$(df --output=source "$DOCKYARD_ROOT" 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | tr -d '[:space:]')" || true
+                if [ -n "$root_dataset" ] && zfs list "$root_dataset" &>/dev/null; then
+                    on_zfs=true
+                    # Destroy all child ZFS datasets (Docker layers, etc.)
+                    local children
+                    children="$(zfs list -r -H -o name "$root_dataset" 2>/dev/null | tail -n +2 | sort -r)" || true
+                    if [ -n "$children" ]; then
+                        echo "Cleaning up ZFS datasets under ${root_dataset}..."
+                        while IFS= read -r ds; do
+                            zfs destroy -f "$ds" 2>/dev/null || true
+                        done <<< "$children"
+                    fi
+                fi
+            fi
+            if [ "$on_zfs" = true ]; then
+                # Can't remove ZFS mountpoint itself; remove contents only
+                rm -rf "${DOCKYARD_ROOT:?}"/* "${DOCKYARD_ROOT}"/.[!.]* 2>/dev/null || true
+                echo "Removed contents of ${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/ (ZFS mountpoint preserved)"
+            else
+                rm -rf "$DOCKYARD_ROOT"
+                echo "Removed ${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/"
+            fi
         fi
     fi

     1→require "net/http"
     2→require "json"
     3→
     4→# Checks whether newer versions of the Sandcastle app and sandbox images are
     5→# available in GHCR. Results are cached for 15 minutes to avoid hammering the
     6→# registry API on every admin page load.
     7→class UpdateChecker
     8→  GHCR_HOST  = "ghcr.io"
     9→  OWNER      = "thieso2"
    10→  APP_REPO   = "sandcastle"
    11→  BOX_REPO   = "sandcastle-sandbox"
    12→  CACHE_KEY  = "update_checker/status"
    13→  CACHE_TTL  = 15.minutes
    14→
    15→  class Error < StandardError; end
    16→
    17→  # Returns cached (or fresh) update status hash.
    18→  def check
    19→    Rails.cache.fetch(CACHE_KEY, expires_in: CACHE_TTL) { perform_check }
    20→  end
    21→
    22→  # Busts the cache and re-checks immediately.
    23→  def force_check
    24→    Rails.cache.delete(CACHE_KEY)
    25→    check
    26→  end
    27→
    28→  private
    29→
    30→  def perform_check
    31→    {
    32→      app:        image_status(APP_REPO, "ghcr.io/#{OWNER}/#{APP_REPO}:latest"),
    33→      sandbox:    image_status(BOX_REPO, "ghcr.io/#{OWNER}/#{BOX_REPO}:latest"),
    34→      checked_at: Time.current
    35→    }
    36→  rescue => e
    37→    Rails.logger.error("UpdateChecker#perform_check failed: #{e.message}")
    38→    { error: e.message, checked_at: Time.current }
    39→  end
    40→
    41→  def image_status(repo, local_ref)
    42→    local_digest  = local_repo_digest(local_ref)
    43→    remote_digest = remote_manifest_digest(repo)
    44→    built_at      = local_image_built_at(local_ref)
    45→
    46→    {
    47→      local_digest:     local_digest,
    48→      remote_digest:    remote_digest,
    49→      built_at:         built_at,
    50→      update_available: local_digest.present? && remote_digest.present? && local_digest != remote_digest
    51→    }
    52→  rescue => e
    53→    Rails.logger.warn("UpdateChecker#image_status(#{repo}) failed: #{e.message}")
    54→    { error: e.message }
    55→  end
    56→
    57→  # Returns the manifest digest stored in the image's RepoDigests (e.g.
    58→  # "sha256:abc123") — the digest it had when pulled from the registry.
    59→  def local_repo_digest(image_ref)
    60→    img = Docker::Image.get(image_ref)
    61→    digests = img.info["RepoDigests"] || []
    62→    digests.first&.split("@")&.last
    63→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError, Docker::Error::DockerError
    64→    nil
    65→  end
    66→
    67→  # Reads BUILD_DATE from image labels/env so we can show "built X ago".
    68→  def local_image_built_at(image_ref)
    69→    img    = Docker::Image.get(image_ref)
    70→    config = img.json.dig("Config") || {}
    71→    labels = config["Labels"] || {}
    72→
    73→    date_str = labels["org.opencontainers.image.created"] ||
    74→               labels["BUILD_DATE"]
    75→
    76→    if date_str.nil?
    77→      env_entry = (config["Env"] || []).find { |e| e.start_with?("BUILD_DATE=") }
    78→      date_str  = env_entry&.split("=", 2)&.last
    79→    end
    80→
    81→    date_str ? Time.zone.parse(date_str) : nil
    82→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError, ArgumentError, TypeError
    83→    nil
    84→  end
    85→
    86→  # Queries GHCR for the latest manifest digest without pulling the image.
    87→  def remote_manifest_digest(repo)
    88→    token=[REDACTED](repo)
    89→    return nil unless token
    90→
    91→    uri = URI("https://#{GHCR_HOST}/v2/#{OWNER}/#{repo}/manifests/latest")
    92→    req = Net::HTTP::Get.new(uri)
    93→    req["Authorization"] = "Bearer #{token}"
    94→    req["Accept"]        = "application/vnd.docker.distribution.manifest.v2+json," \
    95→                           "application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json"
    96→
    97→    response = Net::HTTP.start(uri.hostname, uri.port, use_ssl: true,
    98→                               read_timeout: 10, open_timeout: 5) do |http|
    99→      http.request(req)
   100→    end
   101→
   102→    response["docker-content-digest"] || response["Docker-Content-Digest"]
   103→  rescue => e
   104→    Rails.logger.warn("UpdateChecker: failed to fetch remote digest for #{repo}: #{e.message}")
   105→    nil
   106→  end
   107→
   108→  # GHCR grants anonymous tokens for public repositories.
   109→  def fetch_anonymous_token(repo)
   110→    uri = URI("https://#{GHCR_HOST}/token")
   111→    uri.query = URI.encode_www_form(
   112→      scope:   "repository:#{OWNER}/#{repo}:pull",
   113→      service: GHCR_HOST
   114→    )
   115→    response = Net::HTTP.get_response(uri)
   116→    return nil unless response.is_a?(Net::HTTPSuccess)
   117→
   118→    JSON.parse(response.body)["token"]
   119→  rescue => e
   120→    Rails.logger.warn("UpdateChecker: failed to fetch anonymous token: #{e.message}")
   121→    nil
   122→  end
   123→end
   124→

> AGENT

The problem is clear — GHCR returns an OCI index (multi-arch manifest list), but we're only accepting single-arch manifest types. Need to add the OCI index media type to the Accept header.

> AGENT

Fixed. The issue was that GHCR returns OCI index manifests (multi-arch) for `:latest` tags, but the `Accept` header only included single-manifest types, causing a 404. Added `manifest.list.v2+json` and `oci.image.index.v1+json` to the Accept header. The fix is live on dev.sand now.

> AGENT

It's already in the UI. Go to the **Admin Dashboard** at `https://dev.sand/admin` — there's a "System Update" section at the top with:

1. **"Check for Updates"** button — click this first. It will compare local vs remote image digests.
2. **"Update Now"** button — appears when an update is detected. It pulls new images and restarts the containers.

Now that the fix is live, "Check for Updates" should work and show whether your images are up to date or have updates available.

     1→import { Controller } from "@hotwired/stimulus"
     2→
     3→// Handles "Check for Updates" and "Update Now" buttons on the admin dashboard.
     4→// After triggering an update it polls /api/info until the version string changes,
     5→// then reloads the page so the new version is visible.
     6→export default class extends Controller {
     7→  static targets = ["checkBtn", "updateBtn", "progressMsg"]
     8→  static values  = { checkUrl: String, performUrl: String, pollUrl: String }
     9→
    10→  async checkForUpdates() {
    11→    this.checkBtnTarget.disabled    = true
    12→    this.checkBtnTarget.textContent = "Checking…"
    13→
    14→    try {
    15→      const resp = await fetch(this.checkUrlValue, {
    16→        method:  "GET",
    17→        headers: { "Accept": "application/json", "X-CSRF-Token": this.#csrfToken() }
    18→      })
    19→      if (resp.ok) {
    20→        // Reload the turbo frame to show fresh data
    21→        const frame = this.element.closest("turbo-frame")
    22→        if (frame) frame.reload()
    23→      }
    24→    } finally {
    25→      this.checkBtnTarget.disabled    = false
    26→      this.checkBtnTarget.textContent = "Check for Updates"
    27→    }
    28→  }
    29→
    30→  async performUpdate() {
    31→    if (!confirm("This will pull the latest images and restart the app. Continue?")) return
    32→
    33→    this.updateBtnTarget.disabled    = true
    34→    this.updateBtnTarget.textContent = "Updating…"
    35→    this.progressMsgTarget.classList.remove("hidden")
    36→
    37→    try {
    38→      const resp = await fetch(this.performUrlValue, {
    39→        method:  "POST",
    40→        headers: { "Accept": "application/json", "X-CSRF-Token": this.#csrfToken() }
    41→      })
    42→
    43→      if (resp.ok) {
    44→        // Poll /api/info until the server comes back (with a new or same version)
    45→        await this.#pollUntilReady()
    46→        window.location.reload()
    47→      } else {
    48→        const body = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}))
    49→        alert("Update failed: " + (body.error || resp.statusText))
    50→        this.progressMsgTarget.classList.add("hidden")
    51→        this.updateBtnTarget.disabled    = false
    52→        this.updateBtnTarget.textContent = "Update Now"
    53→      }
    54→    } catch (e) {
    55→      alert("Update request failed: " + e.message)
    56→      this.progressMsgTarget.classList.add("hidden")
    57→      this.updateBtnTarget.disabled    = false
    58→      this.updateBtnTarget.textContent = "Update Now"
    59→    }
    60→  }
    61→
    62→  // Polls the API info endpoint every 3 seconds until it responds successfully,
    63→  // indicating the new container is up.
    64→  async #pollUntilReady(maxAttempts = 60, intervalMs = 3000) {
    65→    for (let i = 0; i < maxAttempts; i++) {
    66→      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, intervalMs))
    67→      try {
    68→        const resp = await fetch(this.pollUrlValue, { headers: { "Accept": "application/json" } })
    69→        if (resp.ok) return
    70→      } catch (_) {
    71→        // Server restarting — keep polling
    72→      }
    73→    }
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  #csrfToken() {
    77→    return document.querySelector("meta[name='csrf-token']")?.content || ""
    78→  }

```
     1→# Orchestrates a self-update: pulls fresh images from GHCR, then spawns a
     2→# short-lived "updater" container that runs `docker compose up -d` from outside
     3→# the web container — necessary because the web container cannot recreate itself
     4→# while it is still running.
     5→class UpdateManager
     6→  APP_IMAGE    = "ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle:latest"
     7→  SANDBOX_IMAGE = SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE
     8→  COMPOSE_PATH  = "/sandcastle/docker-compose.yml"
     9→
    10→  class Error < StandardError; end
    11→
    12→  # Pulls images (app + sandbox by default) and spawns the updater container.
    13→  # The caller's HTTP response will complete before the compose restart kicks in
    14→  # (the updater sleeps 2 s before running compose).
    15→  def perform_update!(pull_app: true, pull_sandbox: true)
    16→    pull_image(APP_IMAGE)     if pull_app
    17→    pull_image(SANDBOX_IMAGE) if pull_sandbox
    18→    spawn_updater_container
    19→  rescue Error
    20→    raise
    21→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    22→    raise Error, "Docker error during update: #{e.message}"
    23→  end
    24→
    25→  private
    26→
    27→  def pull_image(image)
    28→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => image)
    29→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    30→    raise Error, "Failed to pull #{image}: #{e.message}"
    31→  end
    32→
    33→  def spawn_updater_container
    34→    container = Docker::Container.create(
    35→      "Image" => APP_IMAGE,
    36→      "Cmd"   => [ "sh", "-c",
    37→                   "sleep 2 && docker compose -f #{COMPOSE_PATH} up -d" ],
    38→      "HostConfig" => {
    39→        "Binds" => [
    40→          "/var/run/docker.sock:/var/run/docker.sock",
    41→          "/sandcastle:/sandcastle"
    42→        ],
    43→        "AutoRemove" => true
    44→      }
    45→    )
    46→    container.start
    47→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    48→    raise Error, "Failed to spawn updater container: #{e.message}"
    49→  end
    50→end
    51→

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/admin/update_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "    # POST /admin/update/perform — pull images and spawn the updater container\n    def perform\n      UpdateManager.new.perform_update!\n      render json: { status: \"updating\" }\n    rescue UpdateManager::Error => e\n      render json: { error: e.message }, status: :unprocessable_entity\n    end",
  "new_string": "    # POST /admin/update/perform — pull images and spawn the updater container\n    # Optional param: target=sandbox (pull sandbox image only, no app restart)\n    #                 target=app (pull app image + restart)\n    #                 target=all (default — pull both + restart)\n    def perform\n      target = params[:target] || \"all\"\n      pull_app     = %w[all app].include?(target)\n      pull_sandbox = %w[all sandbox].include?(target)\n      restart_app  = pull_app # only restart when the app image changed\n\n      UpdateManager.new.perform_update!(pull_app: pull_app, pull_sandbox: pull_sandbox, restart_app: restart_app)\n      render json: { status: \"updating\", target: target }\n    rescue UpdateManager::Error => e\n      render json: { error: e.message }, status: :unprocessable_entity\n    end"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/update_manager.rb",
  "old_string": "  # Pulls images (app + sandbox by default) and spawns the updater container.\n  # The caller's HTTP response will complete before the compose restart kicks in\n  # (the updater sleeps 2 s before running compose).\n  def perform_update!(pull_app: true, pull_sandbox: true)\n    pull_image(APP_IMAGE)     if pull_app\n    pull_image(SANDBOX_IMAGE) if pull_sandbox\n    spawn_updater_container\n  rescue Error\n    raise\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n    raise Error, \"Docker error during update: #{e.message}\"\n  end",
  "new_string": "  # Pulls images (app + sandbox by default) and optionally restarts the app.\n  # When only the sandbox image is updated, no restart is needed — new sandboxes\n  # will use the freshly pulled image automatically.\n  def perform_update!(pull_app: true, pull_sandbox: true, restart_app: true)\n    pull_image(APP_IMAGE)     if pull_app\n    pull_image(SANDBOX_IMAGE) if pull_sandbox\n    spawn_updater_container   if restart_app\n    Rails.cache.delete(UpdateChecker::CACHE_KEY)\n  rescue Error\n    raise\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n    raise Error, \"Docker error during update: #{e.message}\"\n  end"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/javascript/controllers/update_check_controller.js",
  "content": "import { Controller } from \"@hotwired/stimulus\"\n\n// Handles \"Check for Updates\" and per-image \"Update\" buttons on the admin dashboard.\n// After triggering an app update it polls /api/info until the server comes back,\n// then reloads the page. Sandbox-only updates reload the turbo frame immediately.\nexport default class extends Controller {\n  static targets = [\"checkBtn\", \"progressMsg\", \"progressText\"]\n  static values  = { checkUrl: String, performUrl: String, pollUrl: String }\n\n  async checkForUpdates() {\n    this.checkBtnTarget.disabled    = true\n    this.checkBtnTarget.textContent = \"Checking…\"\n\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch(this.checkUrlValue, {\n        method:  \"GET\",\n        headers: { \"Accept\": \"application/json\", \"X-CSRF-Token\": this.#csrfToken() }\n      })\n      if (resp.ok) {\n        const frame = this.element.closest(\"turbo-frame\")\n        if (frame) frame.reload()\n      }\n    } finally {\n      this.checkBtnTarget.disabled    = false\n      this.checkBtnTarget.textContent = \"Check for Updates\"\n    }\n  }\n\n  async performUpdate(event) {\n    const target = event.currentTarget.dataset.updateTarget || \"all\"\n    const labels = { all: \"all images\", app: \"the app\", sandbox: \"the sandbox image\" }\n    const label  = labels[target] || target\n\n    if (!confirm(`This will pull ${label}${target !== \"sandbox\" ? \" and restart the app\" : \"\"}. Continue?`)) return\n\n    // Disable all update buttons\n    this.element.querySelectorAll(\"[data-action*='performUpdate']\").forEach(btn => {\n      btn.disabled = true\n      btn.classList.add(\"opacity-50\")\n    })\n\n    const restarts = target !== \"sandbox\"\n    this.progressMsgTarget.classList.remove(\"hidden\")\n    if (this.hasProgressTextTarget) {\n      this.progressTextTarget.textContent = restarts\n        ? \"Updating… page will reload automatically when the new version is live.\"\n        : \"Pulling sandbox image…\"\n    }\n\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch(`${this.performUrlValue}?target=${target}`, {\n        method:  \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Accept\": \"application/json\", \"X-CSRF-Token\": this.#csrfToken() }\n      })\n\n      if (resp.ok) {\n        if (restarts) {\n          await this.#pollUntilReady()\n          window.location.reload()\n        } else {\n          // Sandbox-only update — no restart, just refresh the frame\n          if (this.hasProgressTextTarget) {\n            this.progressTextTarget.textContent = \"Sandbox image updated. Refreshing…\"\n          }\n          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1000))\n          const frame = this.element.closest(\"turbo-frame\")\n          if (frame) frame.reload()\n        }\n      } else {\n        const body = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}))\n        alert(\"Update failed: \" + (body.error || resp.statusText))\n        this.#resetButtons()\n      }\n    } catch (e) {\n      alert(\"Update request failed: \" + e.message)\n      this.#resetButtons()\n    }\n  }\n\n  #resetButtons() {\n    this.progressMsgTarget.classList.add(\"hidden\")\n    this.element.querySelectorAll(\"[data-action*='performUpdate']\").forEach(btn => {\n      btn.disabled = false\n      btn.classList.remove(\"opacity-50\")\n    })\n  }\n\n  // Polls the API info endpoint every 3 seconds until it responds successfully.\n  async #pollUntilReady(maxAttempts = 60, intervalMs = 3000) {\n    for (let i = 0; i < maxAttempts; i++) {\n      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, intervalMs))\n      try {\n        const resp = await fetch(this.pollUrlValue, { headers: { \"Accept\": \"application/json\" } })\n        if (resp.ok) return\n      } catch (_) {\n        // Server restarting — keep polling\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  #csrfToken() {\n    return document.querySelector(\"meta[name='csrf-token']\")?.content || \"\"\n  }\n}\n"
}

- **"Update All" button** at the bottom when both have updates
- **Sandbox-only updates** just pull the image without restarting the app (new sandboxes will use it automatically)
- **App updates** pull + restart via the updater container as before

Try clicking "Check for Updates" on dev.sand to see the new buttons.

> AGENT

Here's how it currently works:

1. **POST `/admin/update/perform`** — In a single synchronous HTTP request, it calls `Docker::Image.create` for both images (blocking pull), then spawns an "updater" container
2. **Updater container** — A short-lived container with the Docker socket mounted that runs `sleep 2 && docker compose up -d`, which recreates the web container from outside (since the web container can't restart itself)
3. **JS polling** — After the POST returns, JS polls `/api/info` every 3s until the server responds, then reloads

**Problems:**
- The image pull is synchronous in the HTTP request — can timeout on large images, no progress feedback
- Pull and restart are coupled — no separation
- No visual feedback, just a tiny spinner in the update card

Your proposed flow is much better. Let me implement it:

1. **Pull phase** — Background job pulls images, JS polls for progress
2. **Restart phase** — Once pulled, spawn the updater container  
3. **Fullscreen updating page** — Polls until the app is back, then reloads

    10→  class Error < StandardError; end

    15→  def perform_update!(pull_app: true, pull_sandbox: true, restart_app: true)
    16→    pull_image(APP_IMAGE)     if pull_app

    48→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/services/update_manager.rb",
  "content": "# Orchestrates a self-update: pulls fresh images from GHCR, then spawns a\n# short-lived \"updater\" container that runs `docker compose up -d` from outside\n# the web container — necessary because the web container cannot recreate itself\n# while it is still running.\n#\n# Update flow:\n#   1. POST /admin/update/pull?target=X   → starts background pull, tracks via cache\n#   2. GET  /admin/update/status           → polls pull progress\n#   3. POST /admin/update/restart          → spawns updater container\n#   4. GET  /admin/update/progress         → fullscreen page that polls until app is back\nclass UpdateManager\n  APP_IMAGE     = \"ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle:latest\"\n  SANDBOX_IMAGE = SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE\n  COMPOSE_PATH  = \"/sandcastle/docker-compose.yml\"\n  CACHE_KEY     = \"update_manager/pull_status\"\n\n  class Error < StandardError; end\n\n  # Starts pulling images in a background thread. Progress is tracked in cache.\n  def start_pull(target:)\n    pull_app     = %w[all app].include?(target)\n    pull_sandbox = %w[all sandbox].include?(target)\n\n    write_status(state: \"pulling\", target: target, started_at: Time.current.iso8601)\n\n    Thread.new do\n      begin\n        if pull_app\n          write_status(state: \"pulling\", target: target, step: \"Pulling app image…\")\n          pull_image(APP_IMAGE)\n        end\n\n        if pull_sandbox\n          write_status(state: \"pulling\", target: target, step: \"Pulling sandbox image…\")\n          pull_image(SANDBOX_IMAGE)\n        end\n\n        write_status(state: \"ready\", target: target, step: \"Images pulled. Ready to apply.\")\n      rescue => e\n        write_status(state: \"error\", target: target, step: e.message)\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  # Returns the current pull status hash from cache.\n  def pull_status\n    Rails.cache.read(CACHE_KEY) || { state: \"idle\" }\n  end\n\n  # Spawns the updater container that restarts the app via docker compose.\n  def restart!\n    spawn_updater_container\n    Rails.cache.delete(CACHE_KEY)\n    Rails.cache.delete(UpdateChecker::CACHE_KEY)\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n    raise Error, \"Failed to spawn updater container: #{e.message}\"\n  end\n\n  private\n\n  def write_status(hash)\n    Rails.cache.write(CACHE_KEY, hash, expires_in: 10.minutes)\n  end\n\n  def pull_image(image)\n    Docker::Image.create(\"fromImage\" => image)\n  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e\n    raise Error, \"Failed to pull #{image}: #{e.message}\"\n  end\n\n  def spawn_updater_container\n    container = Docker::Container.create(\n      \"Image\" => APP_IMAGE,\n      \"Cmd\"   => [ \"sh\", \"-c\",\n                   \"sleep 2 && docker compose -f #{COMPOSE_PATH} up -d\" ],\n      \"HostConfig\" => {\n        \"Binds\" => [\n          \"/var/run/docker.sock:/var/run/docker.sock\",\n          \"/sandcastle:/sandcastle\"\n        ],\n        \"AutoRemove\" => true\n      }\n    )\n    container.start\n  end\nend\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/admin/update_controller.rb",
  "content": "module Admin\n  class UpdateController < BaseController\n    # GET /admin/update/check — force-refresh the update status cache and return JSON\n    def check\n      result = UpdateChecker.new.force_check\n      render json: result\n    end\n\n    # POST /admin/update/pull — start pulling images in the background\n    #   target: sandbox | app | all (default)\n    def pull\n      target = params[:target] || \"all\"\n      UpdateManager.new.start_pull(target: target)\n      render json: { status: \"pulling\", target: target }\n    end\n\n    # GET /admin/update/status — poll pull progress\n    def status\n      render json: UpdateManager.new.pull_status\n    end\n\n    # POST /admin/update/restart — spawn the updater container that runs docker compose up\n    def restart\n      UpdateManager.new.restart!\n      render json: { status: \"restarting\" }\n    rescue UpdateManager::Error => e\n      render json: { error: e.message }, status: :unprocessable_entity\n    end\n\n    # GET /admin/update/progress — fullscreen page shown while the app restarts\n    def progress\n      render layout: false\n    end\n  end\nend\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/views/admin/update/progress.html.erb",
  "content": "<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>Updating Sandcastle…</title>\n  <style>\n    *, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }\n    body {\n      font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, \"SF Mono\", Menlo, Consolas, monospace;\n      background: #0f172a;\n      color: #e2e8f0;\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      justify-content: center;\n      min-height: 100vh;\n    }\n    .container {\n      text-align: center;\n      max-width: 28rem;\n      padding: 2rem;\n    }\n    .spinner {\n      width: 3rem; height: 3rem;\n      border: 3px solid #334155;\n      border-top-color: #3b82f6;\n      border-radius: 50%;\n      animation: spin 0.8s linear infinite;\n      margin: 0 auto 1.5rem;\n    }\n    @keyframes spin { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }\n    h1 { font-size: 1.25rem; font-weight: 600; margin-bottom: 0.5rem; }\n    .status { color: #94a3b8; font-size: 0.875rem; margin-bottom: 1.5rem; }\n    .dots::after {\n      content: '';\n      animation: dots 1.5s steps(4, end) infinite;\n    }\n    @keyframes dots {\n      0%  { content: ''; }\n      25% { content: '.'; }\n      50% { content: '..'; }\n      75% { content: '...'; }\n    }\n    .done { color: #22c55e; }\n    .log {\n      margin-top: 1.5rem;\n      text-align: left;\n      font-size: 0.75rem;\n      color: #64748b;\n      max-height: 8rem;\n      overflow-y: auto;\n    }\n    .log div { padding: 0.125rem 0; }\n    .log .time { color: #475569; }\n  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n  <div class=\"container\">\n    <div class=\"spinner\" id=\"spinner\"></div>\n    <h1 id=\"title\">Updating Sandcastle<span class=\"dots\"></span></h1>\n    <p class=\"status\" id=\"status\">Waiting for the new version to come online</p>\n    <div class=\"log\" id=\"log\"></div>\n  </div>\n\n  <script>\n    const pollUrl = \"<%= api_info_path %>\";\n    const dashUrl = \"<%= admin_dashboard_path %>\";\n    const log     = document.getElementById(\"log\");\n    const title   = document.getElementById(\"title\");\n    const status  = document.getElementById(\"status\");\n    const spinner = document.getElementById(\"spinner\");\n    let attempt = 0;\n\n    function addLog(msg) {\n      const now = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();\n      const div = document.createElement(\"div\");\n      div.innerHTML = `<span class=\"time\">${now}</span> ${msg}`;\n      log.appendChild(div);\n      log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;\n    }\n\n    async function poll() {\n      attempt++;\n      try {\n        const resp = await fetch(pollUrl, {\n          headers: { \"Accept\": \"application/json\" },\n          cache: \"no-store\"\n        });\n        if (resp.ok) {\n          addLog(\"New version is live!\");\n          spinner.style.borderTopColor = \"#22c55e\";\n          spinner.style.animation = \"none\";\n          title.innerHTML = \"Update Complete\";\n          title.classList.add(\"done\");\n          status.textContent = \"Redirecting to dashboard…\";\n          setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = dashUrl; }, 1500);\n          return;\n        }\n        addLog(`Attempt ${attempt}: server returned ${resp.status}`);\n      } catch (e) {\n        addLog(`Attempt ${attempt}: waiting for server…`);\n      }\n      setTimeout(poll, 3000);\n    }\n\n    addLog(\"App is restarting…\");\n    // Give the updater container time to start docker compose\n    setTimeout(poll, 5000);\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

text-green-600 font-medium\">Up to date</span>\n            <% end %>\n          </div>\n        <% end %>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <% if any_update %>\n      <div class=\"mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">\n        <% if app.is_a?(Hash) && app[:update_available] && sandbox_img.is_a?(Hash) && sandbox_img[:update_available] %>\n          <button {
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/javascript/controllers/update_check_controller.js",
  "content": "import { Controller } from \"@hotwired/stimulus\"\n\n// Manages the update flow on the admin dashboard:\n//   1. Check for updates (refresh turbo frame)\n//   2. Start pull (POST /pull, then poll /status for progress)\n//   3. When pull is ready: sandbox-only → refresh frame; app → restart + fullscreen progress\nexport default class extends Controller {\n  static targets = [\"checkBtn\", \"pullProgress\", \"pullStatus\"]\n  static values  = {\n    checkUrl:    String,\n    pullUrl:     String,\n    statusUrl:   String,\n    restartUrl:  String,\n    progressUrl: String\n  }\n\n  async checkForUpdates() {\n    this.checkBtnTarget.disabled    = true\n    this.checkBtnTarget.textContent = \"Checking…\"\n\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch(this.checkUrlValue, {\n        method:  \"GET\",\n        headers: { \"Accept\": \"application/json\", \"X-CSRF-Token\": this.#csrfToken() }\n      })\n      if (resp.ok) {\n        const frame = this.element.closest(\"turbo-frame\")\n        if (frame) frame.reload()\n      }\n    } finally {\n      this.checkBtnTarget.disabled    = false\n      this.checkBtnTarget.textContent = \"Check for Updates\"\n    }\n  }\n\n  async startPull(event) {\n    const target = event.currentTarget.dataset.updateTarget || \"all\"\n    const labels = { all: \"all images\", app: \"the app image\", sandbox: \"the sandbox image\" }\n\n    if (!confirm(`Pull ${labels[target] || target}?`)) return\n\n    // Disable all update buttons\n    this.element.querySelectorAll(\"[data-action*='startPull']\").forEach(btn => {\n      btn.disabled = true\n      btn.classList.add(\"opacity-50\")\n    })\n\n    // Show pull progress\n    this.pullProgressTarget.classList.remove(\"hidden\")\n    this.pullStatusTarget.textContent = \"Starting pull…\"\n\n    try {\n      const resp = await fetch(`${this.pullUrlValue}?target=${target}`, {\n        method:  \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Accept\": \"application/json\", \"X-CSRF-Token\": this.#csrfToken() }\n      })\n\n      if (!resp.ok) {\n        const body = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}))\n        throw new Error(body.error || resp.statusText)\n      }\n\n      // Poll for pull completion\n      await this.#pollPullStatus(target)\n    } catch (e) {\n      alert(\"Update failed: \" + e.message)\n      this.pullProgressTarget.classList.add(\"hidden\")\n      this.element.querySelectorAll(\"[data-action*='startPull']\").forEach(btn => {\n        btn.disabled = false\n        btn.classList.remove(\"opacity-50\")\n      })\n    }\n  }\n\n  async #pollPullStatus(target) {\n    const needsRestart = target !== \"sandbox\"\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {  // up to 6 minutes\n      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2000))\n\n      try {\n        const resp = await fetch(this.statusUrlValue, {\n          headers: { \"Accept\": \"application/json\" },\n          cache: \"no-store\"\n        })\n        if (!resp.ok) continue\n\n        const data = await resp.json()\n        this.pullStatusTarget.textContent = data.step || data.state || \"Pulling…\"\n\n        if (data.state === \"ready\") {\n          if (needsRestart) {\n            this.pullStatusTarget.textContent = \"Images pulled. Restarting app…\"\n            await this.#triggerRestart()\n          } else {\n            // Sandbox-only — just refresh the frame\n            this.pullStatusTarget.textContent = \"Sandbox image updated!\"\n            await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1000))\n            const frame = this.element.closest(\"turbo-frame\")\n            if (frame) frame.reload()\n          }\n          return\n        }\n\n        if (data.state === \"error\") {\n          throw new Error(data.step || \"Pull failed\")\n        }\n      } catch (e) {\n        if (e.message && !e.message.includes(\"fetch\")) throw e\n      }\n    }\n\n    throw new Error(\"Pull timed out\")\n  }\n\n  async #triggerRestart() {\n    const resp = await fetch(this.restartUrlValue, {\n      method:  \"POST\",\n      headers: { \"Accept\": \"application/json\", \"X-CSRF-Token\": this.#csrfToken() }\n    })\n\n    if (!resp.ok) {\n      const body = await resp.json().catch(() => ({}))\n      throw new Error(body.error || \"Restart failed\")\n    }\n\n    // Navigate to the fullscreen progress page\n    window.location.href = this.progressUrlValue\n  }\n\n  #csrfToken() {\n    return document.querySelector(\"meta[name='csrf-token']\")?.content || \"\"\n  }\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Deployed. Here's the new update flow:

**1. Check for Updates** — click the button, shows which images have updates

**2. Pull** — click "Update Sandbox Image", "Update App", or "Update All". This starts the pull in a background thread with a progress indicator showing the current step ("Pulling app image…", "Pulling sandbox image…")

**3a. Sandbox-only** — when pull finishes, the frame refreshes immediately. No restart needed — new sandboxes automatically use the fresh image.

**3b. App update** — when pull finishes, it spawns the updater container and redirects to a fullscreen dark progress page that:
- Shows a spinner and "Updating Sandcastle…"
- Logs each poll attempt with timestamps
- Polls `/api/info` every 3s until the new app responds
- Shows "Update Complete" in green, then redirects to the admin dashboard

Try it on dev.sand now — click "Check for Updates" then use one of the update buttons.
